The upcoming runoff election on October 25 pits leftist President Luiz Inácio Lula da Silva against Flavio Bolsonaro, who is expected to continue his father's policies if victorious.

 - Deforestation rates in Brazil's Amazon have dropped to the lowest levels since records began in 1988.
 - The presidential election is crucial as candidates represent vastly different views on environmental policies.

The International Bureau of Weights and Measurements has announced that starting May 2027, leap seconds will no longer be added to Coordinated Universal Time, effectively ending a long-standing practice that has caused complications for various technologies reliant on precise timekeeping. This decision is influenced by increasing concerns over the potential need for a negative leap second due to Earth's accelerating rotational speed, which has been partially attributed to climate change. As a result, this change aims to simplify timekeeping for technology systems worldwide.

 - Leap seconds will stop being added to Coordinated Universal Time starting May 2027.
 - The decision is influenced by concerns about potential negative leap seconds due to changes in Earth's rotation.

Source: https://www.wired.com/story/leap-second-is-abolished-bipm-says/

---

The decision was influenced by concerns over the emerging possibility of a negative leap second, which could arise from the Earth's changing rotation speed linked to climate change. This change aims to simplify timekeeping for systems worldwide.

 - Leap seconds, used to align atomic time with astronomical time, will no longer be issued starting in 2027.
 - The elimination of leap seconds aims to reduce complications for technology systems that rely on precise timekeeping.
